Strains

152 BacDive strains classified here.

NCBI assemblies appear first, followed by GTDB, BV-BRC/PATRIC, IMG and AllTheBacteria genome records. Links retain evidence from BacDive, GTDB metadata and GOLD; AllTheBacteria links preserve the BioSample evidence connecting the assembly to the strain. Supplied accession versions, source strain identifiers and database meanings are preserved. NCBI accessions without a version suffix have unspecified version. Links under one strain do not establish genome equivalence; source descriptions may identify a substrain or plasmid. Samples and GOLD organism, sequencing-project and analysis-project records are listed separately. The complete NCBI assembly inventory, other genome-record inventory, related-record inventory and AllTheBacteria browser include strains omitted from this listing.
